Step 1.1 chunking language remains in effect only when Step 1.2.5 activation conditions are not met.\n\n### Step 1.4 — Stage 1 Gate\n\nAll must pass before marking Stage 1 complete:\n\n| Gate | Requirement |\n|------|-------------|\n| G1 | File inventory table covers all diff entries |\n| G2 | Every distinct function scope and top-level declaration in every `@@` hunk has its own CC entry; `DEL` and `TABLE` constructs always get standalone CCs (per 2nd-pass rules of active REVIEW_PATH) |\n| G3 | Every `.c`/`.h` has INF resolved or `INF: ⚠️ not found` |\n| G4 | ELINK table present or noted as skipped |\n| G5 | Call graph present or noted as skipped |\n| G6 | CC Registry complete in context memory — all files and constructs registered |\n| G7 | CC Registry persisted — `$SESSION_DIR/cc_registry.md` when `RUNTIME_MODE = DISK`; context memory when `MEMORY-ONLY` (see Step 1.1.2 and Constraint precedence) |\n| G8 | PCD cache table and ELINK cache table both present in context memory (or explicitly noted as `[SKIPPED]` or `[PCD-CACHE: SKIPPED — no SDL/KEY/PCD content in diff]`) |\n| G9 | Unit test review mode resolved — `IS_UNIT_TEST_REVIEW` recorded in context memory (`TRUE` or `FALSE`) |\n| G10 | Chunk Plan written to `cc_registry.md` header with all chunk rows at `pending` status and `ACTIVE_CHUNK = C1` recorded in context memory — OR — `[CHUNK-PLAN: SKIPPED]` recorded when activation conditions are not met |\n\n**Unit test review mode (G9) — explicit user tag required:**\n\n`IS_UNIT_TEST_REVIEW = TRUE` when **either** of the following is true:\n1. The user's initial review request contains one of the following inline tags (case-insensitive):\n   - `[unit-test]`\n   - `--unit-test`\n   - `unit test review`\n   - `review unit test`\n   - `unit-test-review`\n2. Step 0.2C resolved `ACTIVE_TAGS` to include `Unittesting` (i.e., the prompt contained `SourceTag: Unittesting`).\n\n**Default when neither condition is met:** `IS_UNIT_TEST_REVIEW = FALSE` — even if the diff contains `*UnitTestHost.c`, `*UnitTestMock.c`, or other unit test files. Presence of unit test files in the diff does NOT auto-activate this mode.\n\n**When `TRUE` via inline tag:** Record `[UNIT-TEST-REVIEW: ENABLED — inline tag detected: \"<matched tag>\"]` in context memory.\n**When `TRUE` via SourceTag:** Record `[UNIT-TEST-REVIEW: ENABLED — SourceTag: Unittesting resolved at Step 0.2C]` in context memory.\n**When `FALSE`:** Record `[UNIT-TEST-REVIEW: DISABLED — no user tag in request]` in context memory. Step 3.10 will be skipped entirely.\n\nThis value is immutable for the remainder of the session.\n\n**Spot-check requirement:** Before leaving Stage 1, verify at least 3 cached entries from different categories. Record `Stage 1 Spot Checks: <passed>/<checked>` in context memory.\n\n### Step 1.5 — Context Memory Contract\n\nA missing **Required** record → abort and restart Stage 1. Stage 2 Cache Verification and Step 3.0 both verify this list before proceeding.\n\n**Required (Stage 1 → Stage 2):**\n- File entry (one per diff file): #, path, STATUS, ±lines, INF, MODULE_TYPE, Priority\n- CC entry (one per construct): CC ID, File, Category, Symbol, Lines, Description\n- INF-DELTA entry (empty table if none): File, API Call, Expected GUID, INF Section, Status\n- Agent State: Stage, FILE#/total, FINDINGS C/M/m/I, FP\n- Resume State: current stage, next CC or file pointer, last valid checkpoint *(inferred from `cc_registry.md` Review Status and `<!-- end-file-N -->` anchors in `Detailed_Review_Report.md`; no standalone resume record is stored)*\n- Chunk Plan state: `ACTIVE_CHUNK = C<N>` and immutable chunk boundaries (or `[CHUNK-PLAN: SKIPPED]`) — required when `REVIEW_PATH = Full` AND reviewable CCs > 80\n\n**Optional (emit fallback tag if missing — continue only when the current CC/IDV does not depend on this cache):**\n- PCD cache entry → `[PCD-CACHE MISSING]`\n- ELINK cache entry → `[ELINK-CACHE MISSING]`\n- Call graph entry → `[CALL GRAPH NOT BUILT]`\n\n**Required (Stage 2 → Stage 3):**\n- Agent State totals (C/M/m/I counts) — in context memory\n- INF-DELTA reachability notes (`[INF-DELTA-CONTEXT-REACHABLE: ...]`) — in context memory\n- All per-file finding sections flushed to `Detailed_Review_Report.md` — on disk (or equivalent content in context memory when `RUNTIME_MODE = MEMORY-ONLY`)\n- All CC `Review Status` values updated in `cc_registry.md` — on disk (or equivalent content in context memory when `RUNTIME_MODE = MEMORY-ONLY`)\n\n---\n\n## Stage 2: Per-CC Code Review\n\n> **Scope:** Review each CC from Stage 1 context against UEFI 2.10+/PI 1.8+/EDK II 2.2+.\n\n> 🧪 **Unit test mode — activate when `IS_UNIT_TEST_REVIEW = TRUE` (resolved at Step 0.2C via `SourceTag: Unittesting`, or at Stage 1 Gate G9 via inline tag):** When the user has explicitly requested unit test review, the following **must not be flagged** as violations during CC evaluation — these are valid and mandatory unit test patterns:\n> - `MODULE_TYPE = HOST_APPLICATION` — valid; no `[Depex]` required; not a firmware module\n> - `.cpp` source files — valid for GoogleTest; C++ idioms, constructors, and templates are allowed\n> - `extern \"C\" {}` wrappers around EDK2 `#include` directives in `.cpp` files — **mandatory** pattern; never flag\n> - `UT_ASSERT_*`, `will_return()`, `expect_value()`, `check_expected()`, `EXPECT_CALL()`, `TEST()`, `TEST_F()` — standard framework calls from `UnitTestLib` / cmocka / GoogleTest\n> - `#include <cmocka.h>`, `#include <Library/GoogleTestLib.h>`, `#include <Library/FunctionMockLib.h>` — framework dependency includes; not unknown symbols\n> - `MOCK_INTERFACE_DECLARATION`, `MOCK_INTERFACE_DEFINITION`, `MOCK_FUNCTION_DECLARATION`, `MOCK_FUNCTION_DEFINITION` — `FunctionMockLib` macros; not violations\n> - `#ifndef CONSTANT / #define CONSTANT value / #endif` guards in `*UnitTestHost.c` for Token.h constants — correct replacement pattern (Rule #6)\n> - Missing `[Depex]` in a `HOST_APPLICATION` INF — valid; `[Depex]` is not applicable to this module type\n> - No `EFIAPI` on C++ test functions — valid; C++ uses its own linkage convention\n>\n> **Severity cap:** All `HOST_APPLICATION` unit test file findings are capped at **MAJOR** maximum. Do not assign `CRITICAL` to unit test code unless confirmed that the module is included in a firmware binary (verify FDF/DSC inclusion chain before escalating). The `/unit-test-review` skill is loaded in Stage 3 Step 3.10 for comprehensive unit test standards validation.\n\n### Step 2.0 — Stage 2 Initialization & Per-File Flush Protocol\n\n**Cache Verification:** Validate all **Required** records per §1.5. Missing required → abort to Stage 1. Missing optional → emit fallback tag per §1.5; if the current CC depends on it, resume Stage 1 for that cache only. Stage 2 must not re-derive caller lists, ELINK prototypes, or PCD values on its own.\n\nIf recovery data exists, resume from the newest valid Stage 2 checkpoint instead of restarting from the first file.\n\n**Execution order:** By file priority (P1 first), then by CC sequence within each file.\n\n**Chunk isolation — applies only when `[CHUNK-PLAN: REGISTERED]`:** Load only the CC entries for `ACTIVE_CHUNK` into active context. Do not load CC details from other chunks. After all files in `ACTIVE_CHUNK` are fully reviewed (Phase A → B → C complete for every file in the chunk):\n1. Execute the normal per-file flush sequence for each file in the chunk.\n2. Update `cc_registry.md` Review Status for every CC in `ACTIVE_CHUNK` (one batched write per file, as normal).\n3. Compress all in-memory CC details for `ACTIVE_CHUNK` to a single aggregate line: `Chunk C<N> (files #<A>–#<B>): <c>C/<m>M/<n>m/<i>I — flushed`.\n4. Update the Chunk Plan header in `cc_registry.md`: set `ACTIVE_CHUNK` row status to `done`.\n5. Record `[CHUNK-COMPLETE: C<N> — files #<A>–#<B>, <c>C/<m>M/<n>m/<i>I]` in context memory.\n6. Advance `ACTIVE_CHUNK` to the next `pending` chunk. Load that chunk's CC entries from `cc_registry.md` on disk. Continue.\n\n**Cross-chunk findings:** When a finding's IDV Step 2 requires evidence from a file in a different chunk (already flushed), tag the finding `[CROSS-CHUNK: C<A>↔C<B> — <symbol or brief description>]`, record with `Confidence: MEDIUM`, and continue. Do not delay writing the finding. Stage 3 Step 3.1.0 resolves these.\n\n**Interrupt recovery within chunk:** Resume from the first `cc_registry.md` entry with `Review Status = NA` within `ACTIVE_CHUNK`. Do not restart from C1.\n\n**Before each file:** Re-read its Stage 1 entry (MODULE_TYPE, INF, priority) from the **context memory CC registry**.\n\nMismatch between cached and *passively observed* MODULE_TYPE (encountered during Phase A reads, not actively re-derived) → record as MAJOR finding `[⚠️ TYPE-DRIFT]` and continue using the more-restrictive of the two types.\n\n**Per-file flush sequence:** After all CCs for a single file are reviewed (Phase A → B → C complete for that file), perform the flush sequence before moving to the next file:\n\n1. **First file only — must** create `Detailed_Review_Report.md` with the full preamble — commit/patch header (from Stage 1 Step 1.0 data), Agent State block (running totals), and file inventory table (from Stage 1 cache). The full CC registry lives in `cc_registry.md` only — **do not** duplicate it in `Detailed_Review_Report.md`. Then **must** append this file's finding section below the preamble.\n2. **All files — must** append this file's finding section to `Detailed_Review_Report.md`. The section format: `## File #N: <path>` header, then CRITICAL/MAJOR/MINOR findings in severity order, then an `#### Informational Notes` subsection for INFO items. **Must terminate every per-file section with the HTML anchor `<!-- end-file-N -->`** (where N is the file's inventory number) — this anchor is required for Stage 3.1.7 INF-DELTA late finding insertion and interrupt recovery. **Must not** use `create_file` on this existing document — doing so overwrites all prior findings. *Note: review documents must not be processed by Markdown tools that strip HTML comments; doing so will break late-finding insertion and interrupt recovery.*\n3. **Must** update `cc_registry.md` — change `Review Status` from `NA` to the result for each CC of this file (one batched write per file, not per individual CC).\n4. Compress this file's in-memory CC detail to a single aggregate line: `File #N <path>: <c>C/<m>M/<n>m/<i>I — flushed`. **Must not** discard finding records from context memory before they are flushed.\n5. **Must** increment `FILE: #<N>/<total>` in Agent State.\n\n**Flush applies in all `RUNTIME_MODE` states** (see Review Document for `RUNTIME_MODE` definition).\n\n**Token budget — context compression:** For patches >30 files or >25 open findings, after each per-file flush compress all `[✅ PASS]` CC entries for the just-flushed file to the aggregate line above. **Never discard finding records.** If total in-memory CC detail entries exceed 200, or the current file has more than 25 open (uncompressed) findings in memory, immediately flush all completed CCs for the current file so far and update `cc_registry.md` for those CCs, then tag the event as `[CONTEXT-FLUSH: mid-file at CC <ID>]` and continue with the remaining CCs of that file. **Override when `[CHUNK-PLAN: REGISTERED]`:** PASS compression is governed by chunk boundaries only (Step 3 of the chunk isolation sequence above) — the `>30 files` and `>25 open findings` compression triggers are superseded and must not fire mid-chunk.\n\n### Step 2.1 — Per-CC Review Protocol\n\nFor each CC entry (one at a time, in priority order):\n\n#### Phase A — Read Context:\n1. Read the modified lines identified in CC registry plus surrounding context. For the **first hunk** of each `.c`/`.h`/`.inf`/`.dec` file, extend the read range to cover at least line 20 to capture the copyright and `@file` block:\n   - `FUNC-DEF`: read the **full function body** + caller sites from Call Graph when a graph is required (all inner changes — local vars, macros, includes, comments — are covered by this read)\n   - `STRUCT`/`TABLE`: read full declaration + all references in the same file\n   - `DEL`: read the deleted code and the surrounding code that remains\n   - `FILE-SCOPE`: read lines ±10 around the change or the enclosing file-scope block\n   - `.inf`/`.sdl`/`.dec`/`.fdf`/`.vfr`/`.asl`: read the full changed section\n2. For P1/P2 files: also read INF `[Sources]`/`[LibraryClasses]` and call sites only when the finding depends on cross-module ownership, lifecycle, or boundary analysis.\n\n**Phase A read budget:** Aim to keep each CC read within a practical range. If a required read is truncated or times out, record `[PHASE-A-TRUNCATED]` or `[PHASE-A-TIMEOUT]`, reduce confidence as appropriate, and continue unless the missing content is required for a blocking finding.\n\n#### Phase B — Review Every Modified Line:\n\n> Apply Pre-Finding Gate (see Constraints section) before opening any finding.\n\nReview each modified line in dimension order. Engineering judgment — not a mechanical checklist; apply the severity model to any suspicious pattern not explicitly listed.\n\n**Review Dimensions §1–§13:** Apply the dimensions defined in the `## Review Dimensions §1–§13` section of this agent. All `§<N>` references resolve to that section.\n\n#### Phase C — Accumulate per-CC in Context Memory; Flush Per-File to Disk:\n\nAfter each individual CC — before moving to the next CC — **must** record the result in context memory. After **all CCs for a file** are complete, **must** execute the per-file flush sequence (Step 2.0).\n\nAfter each individual CC:\n\n1. **Must record in context memory** for every CC, one at a time:\n   - If no issues: mark CC as `[✅ PASS]` with one-line summary in memory\n   - If issues found: record each finding using the format below\n2. **Must** mark CC status in memory: `[✅ PASS]` / `[🔷 MINOR-A]` / `[🔷 MINOR-B]` / `[🔶 MAJOR]` / `[🔴 CRITICAL]` / `[🛑 CRITICAL – ABORT TRIGGER]`\n3. **Must** update Agent State counts in context memory: `FINDINGS: <C>C/<M>M/<m>m/<I>I`. When an IDV verdict = `FALSE POSITIVE` is recorded: **must** also increment `FP: N` in Agent State immediately.\n4. **INF-DELTA context reachability** *(INF-DELTA Pipeline — Stage 2: Reachability Annotation)*: For `FUNC-DEF` CCs where a `[INF-DELTA-SCOPE: context]` entry exists for the same enclosing function, and the Phase A read confirmed the API call is reachable from the entry point of that function, **must** record `[INF-DELTA-CONTEXT-REACHABLE: <API> called in <FunctionName>]` in context memory. Stage 3 Step 3.1.7 consumes this note to promote that entry beyond LOW confidence.\n\n##### Finding Formats:\n\n> **Location links:** In every finding, populate the `Location` field as a Markdown link.\n> - Use the **Resolved Line** from the CC Registry. For `git diff`/staged/uncommitted inputs this is the `+L<N>` hunk offset; for commit/patch inputs it is back-filled from the Stage 2 Phase A function-body read.\n> - Format: `` `FunctionName()` [<basename>:<line>](../../<repo-root-relative/path/file.c>#L<line>) ``\n> - The `../../` prefix is required because the report lives at `.github/reviews/` — two directory levels below (nested within) the workspace root; `../../` navigates up to the workspace root where source files reside (e.g., a file at `AmiModulePkg/Foo/Bar.c` line 47 → `` `Bar()` [Bar.c:47](../../AmiModulePkg/Foo/Bar.c#L47) ``).\n> - **Accuracy check (verify for all findings before advancing to the next CC):** Confirm (a) the link path exactly matches the file's workspace-relative path from the diff header, (b) the `#L<line>` number equals the Resolved Line recorded for that CC in the registry — not the diff hunk context offset, (c) the display text `<basename>:<line>` uses the filename only (no directory), and (d) `FunctionName()` matches the enclosing function where the changed line resides.\n> - If Resolved Line is still blank when writing a finding, read the relevant lines of the file now (as part of the mandatory Phase A context read) and record the line before proceeding. Do NOT emit a finding with a placeholder or guessed line number.\n\nCRITICAL / MAJOR / MINOR:\n```\n#### Finding: [F<N>] – [SHORT TITLE]\n- **File:** `path` | **Severity:** [SEV] | **Confidence:** HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW\n- **Location:** `FunctionName()` [file.c:N](../../relative/path/to/file.c#LN)\n- **Observation:** [what is wrong]\n- **Violation:** [\\<Spec Name\\> §X.Y.Z — \\<normative rule text\\>; for AMI/project conventions: \\<convention name and source\\>]\n- **Fix / Improvement / Enhancement:** [concrete action — verb phrase; state exact change required, e.g., \"Add bounds check before CopyMem at line 47\"; mark `[ARCH-CHANGE REQUIRED]` if redesign needed; mark `[ENHANCEMENT]` for non-blocking hardening suggestions]\n<details><summary>IDV: F<N></summary>\n1. Context read → function body · call sites · INF [Protocols/LibraryClasses/Pcd/PcdEx] · PCD gate · MODULE_TYPE check\n2. Cross-module scope → <search results or N/A>\n3. Spec verdict: CONFIRMED / FALSE POSITIVE / UNVERIFIED\n</details>\n```\n\nINFO: `ℹ️ [F<N>] <path> — description. Suggestion: action.` (batch under `#### Informational Notes`, placed at the end of the containing section after all CRITICAL/MAJOR/MINOR findings)\n\n##### IDV Protocol (Inline Deep Verification):\n\n**Must** be executed for every CRITICAL and MAJOR finding before that finding is recorded — skipping IDV on any CRITICAL or MAJOR is a Prohibited Action. **MINOR-A** findings must use IDV-Lite by default; escalate to full IDV only when the finding crosses a trust boundary, depends on external ownership, or cannot be resolved locally. **MINOR-B** findings must skip IDV entirely — record with one-line rationale and tag `[🔷 MINOR-B]`; no IDV block permitted. INFO: no IDV required.\n\n**Abort Trigger findings:** self-confirming — Step 3 verdict is always `CONFIRMED {spec: UEFI 2.10+ / PI 1.8+}`. Steps 1 and 2 still run to establish `Confidence: HIGH`.\n\nFixed 3-step template — execute in order, no skipping:\n1. **Context read** — confirm you have read: (a) full function body, (b) call sites from Call Graph, (c) INF `[Protocols]`/`[LibraryClasses]`/`[Pcd]`/`[PcdEx]`. If any unread, read now before step 2. Additionally: (d) if changed hunk contains `#if TOKEN` / `#if PCD` guards, verify the resolved value from Stage 1 PCD cache — if the guarded path is compiled out in the build configuration, mark `[GATE-RESOLVED: PCD/TOKEN]` and do NOT open the finding; (e) compare the MODULE_TYPE observed during this read to the Stage 1 cached value — if they differ, record `[TYPE-DRIFT]` and use the more-restrictive of the two types for Step 3.\n   - **Gate after Step 1:** If the live context shows the finding is out of scope for the module type or build configuration, close it as `FALSE POSITIVE` immediately and skip Steps 2–3.\n2. **Cross-module scope** — apply the sub-rule that matches the finding type. **Sub-rule priority:** when a finding spans multiple categories, apply the most restrictive sub-rule only (Security–SMM > Security–intra-module > Lifecycle > ELINK > VFR/ASL > all other non-security). Never stack multiple sub-rules on the same finding.\n   - **Security — SMM / Standalone MM boundary / protocol / auth** (SMM isolation, Standalone MM isolation, `EFI_MM_READY_TO_LOCK_PROTOCOL`, protocol install/uninstall, `VariableLock`, image verification bypass, phase constraints, DMA): `deep-search` cross-package consumers via protocol GUID. Dynamic caller cap by protocol criticality:\n     - **P1 security protocols** (SMM dispatch, MM dispatch, Secure Boot, auth, capsule validation, `EFI_SECURITY_ARCH_PROTOCOL`, `EFI_SECURITY2_ARCH_PROTOCOL`): cap at 5 callers\n     - **P2 standard protocols** (all others): cap at 3 callers\n     - If cap hit → `[CALL GRAPH CAPPED: <actual>/<cap>]`, `Confidence: MEDIUM`. Timeout → `[⚠️ CALL GRAPH MAY BE INCOMPLETE]`, `Confidence: MEDIUM`, advance to step 3.\n   - **Security — intra-module memory safety** (`AllocatePool`/`FreePool` pairs, `CreateEvent`/`CloseEvent`, `OpenProtocol`/`CloseProtocol` within the same module): `grep_search` across the module directory for the complementary operation on the same pointer, GUID, or handle (same procedure as the Lifecycle sub-rule below). **NULL dereference FP guard:** for a missing NULL check on a parameter or return value, additionally scan all call sites of the enclosing function — if every reachable call path guarantees a non-NULL value, close as `FALSE POSITIVE` and tag `[NULL-DEREF-CALLSITE-VERIFIED: pointer always non-NULL at dereference point]`.\n   - **Security — other intra-function** (bounds checks, flag validation, correctness issues with no cross-module consumer path): `N/A — intra-module scope`.\n   - **Lifecycle** (`AllocatePool`/`AllocatePages`, `CreateEvent`/`CreateEventEx`, `InstallProtocolInterface`, `AppendDevicePath`/`DuplicateDevicePath` with no local complementary release): `grep_search` workspace for the complementary op (`FreePool`/`FreePages`, `CloseEvent`, `UninstallProtocolInterface`) on the same pointer, GUID, or handle. **Found** → `[LIFECYCLE-PARTNER-FOUND: <file>:<line>]` → `FALSE POSITIVE` (if partner file is also in the diff, verify the partner's release paths are correct before accepting FALSE POSITIVE). **Not found** → `CONFIRMED`. **Ambiguous** (multiple unrelated matches) → `UNVERIFIED`.\n   - **ELINK**: `grep_search` for the registered function definition; compare its signature to the ELINK cache prototype. Mismatch → `CONFIRMED`. Cache entry `[NOT FOUND]` → `UNVERIFIED — [ELINK-PROTOTYPE-UNVERIFIED: manual build verification required]`.\n   - **VFR `efivarstore` lock**: `grep_search` workspace for `RequestToLock` with matching variable name or GUID. Found → `FALSE POSITIVE`. Not found → `CONFIRMED`.\n   - **ASL/FDF**: classify as Security if the finding involves an FV boundary, SMRAM region, MMRAM region, or the file path contains `Smm`/`StandaloneMm`/`SecureBoot`/`Auth` — apply Security sub-rule. Otherwise → `N/A — intra-module scope`.\n   - **All other non-security**: `N/A — intra-module scope`.\n   - **Gate after Step 2:** If search results are incomplete, ambiguous, or timed out, continue to Step 3 with `Confidence: MEDIUM` unless Step 1 already resolved the finding.\n3. **Spec determination** — choose exactly one verdict:\n   - `CONFIRMED {spec: <spec> §X.Y.Z}` — normatively prohibited.\n   - `FALSE POSITIVE — <reason>` — permitted or valid for MODULE_TYPE. Increment `FP: N`. If the finding was upgraded via a normative SHALL, record `[SHALL-UPGRADE-SUPPRESSED: <reason>]` and revert severity to the pre-upgrade level.\n   - `⚠️ UNVERIFIED — manual check recommended` — `Confidence: MEDIUM`. Do NOT upgrade severity.\n\n\n##### IDV-Lite for MINOR-A:\n1. Context read — confirm the changed code and local surrounding logic were read.\n2. Spec determination — cite the relevant spec or project rule and classify as `CONFIRMED`, `FALSE POSITIVE`, or `UNVERIFIED`.\n3. Escalate to full IDV only when the finding crosses a trust boundary, depends on ownership outside the current module, or cannot be resolved from local context.\n\n##### Lite REVIEW_PATH IDV Scope: Step 2 = same-file callers only (`.c`/`.h`), or same-package `grep_search` for consumers of the changed SDL token / ELINK / INF section (non-`.c`/`.h` files). Lifecycle Partner Search still applies. Use full IDV for CRITICAL/MAJOR; IDV-Lite for MINOR-A unless a boundary-crossing condition forces escalation.\n##### Scaling for Large Reviews (25+ Findings): Group findings by module (same `.c`/`.h` file or same package). Within each group: Step 1 (context read) = read the enclosing function body **once**, shared across all findings in that function; Step 2 (cross-module scope) = run one `deep-search` per unique protocol GUID or lifecycle symbol, shared across all findings citing that GUID/symbol; Step 3 (spec determination) = individual verdict **per finding** — never share verdicts across findings. Record the shared read/search result once and reference it by note in each finding's IDV block (e.g., `\"shared search — see F<N> IDV Step 2\"`).\n\n**Stage 2 checkpoint state** is maintained via the per-file flush to `Detailed_Review_Report.md` and per-file `cc_registry.md` Review Status updates. No separate `s2_checkpoint.md` file is required or written. Crash recovery uses `cc_registry.md` Review Status = `NA` as the resume pointer (see Interrupt Recovery).\n\n### Step 2.2 — Cross-File Consistency\n\n**GUID uniqueness check:** Cross-reference any new GUID literal definitions (i.e., `+` hunk lines matching `{ 0x[0-9a-fA-F]` pattern) in the diff against all workspace `.dec` files using `grep_search`. A collision where the same GUID value is registered under two different names → CRITICAL. Skip and mark `[GUID-CHECK: SKIPPED — no new GUID literals in diff]` when no such lines exist.\n\n- Macro consistency (same `#define`, different values → MAJOR)\n- Cross-module ABI: struct changes → verify field offsets preserved or consumers updated, `sizeof()` assumptions hold\n- Protocol struct changes: function pointer reorder → CRITICAL unless GUID changed\n- Copyright: note if 3+ files share same violation\n\n**Cross-file finding attribution:** Findings from this step are written to a dedicated **`## Cross-File Findings`** section in `Detailed_Review_Report.md`, placed after all per-file sections. Each finding must list all implicated files. Finding IDs continue sequentially from the last per-file `F<N>`. When multiple files are implicated, pin the finding to the highest-priority file (P1 > P2 > P3) for verdict scoring.\n\n### Step 2.3 — Stage 2 Gate\n\n**All must pass:**\n- All files processed (Agent State `FILE: #<total>/<total>` reached)\n- **No P1 file skipped** — files in P1 (SMM/SMRAM/StandaloneMM/MMRAM/SecureBoot/Crypto) must be fully reviewed with all CCs recorded; a single skipped P1 file unconditionally fails this gate\n- Skipped files ≤ 10% of total non-binary, non-excluded files\n- All CC entries reviewed and status recorded in context memory (no `[PENDING]` CC entries)\n- All changed hunks read, all function bodies read\n\nCoverage: `| Hunks: total/reviewed/skipped | CCs: total/reviewed |`\n\n**After gate passes:** All per-file finding sections must have been flushed to `Detailed_Review_Report.md` and all CC Review Status values must have been updated in `cc_registry.md`. **Must** verify `cc_registry.md` has no remaining `NA` Review Status entries (other than `EXCLUDED` CCs) — any remaining `NA` entries **must** cause a return to Stage 2 for those CCs before Stage 3 begins. Stage 3 reads findings from `Detailed_Review_Report.md` and CC statuses from `cc_registry.md` directly — context memory need only hold running Agent State totals and INF-DELTA reachability notes.\n\n---\n\n## Stage 3: Decision\n\n> Scope: Verify per-file sections in `Detailed_Review_Report.md`, append verdict/score/fix sections, then emit verdict. (→ Stage 3 Invariant)\n\n### Step 3.0 — Verify and Complete Detailed_Review_Report Document\n\n**Prerequisite:** **Must** verify `Detailed_Review_Report.md` exists and contains all per-file finding sections (one `## File #N:` section per non-excluded, non-skipped file). If a file section is missing, **must** resume Stage 2 for that file before proceeding. If the document does not exist at all, **must** resume Stage 2 from the first CC with `Review Status = NA` in `cc_registry.md` — the first-file flush will create the document. **Must not** proceed to Step 3.1 until this prerequisite is fully satisfied. When `RUNTIME_MODE = MEMORY-ONLY`, the prerequisite is satisfied by the equivalent content being maintained in context memory; skip the file-existence check. *Note: the verification in this step is a defensive re-check of the Stage 2 Gate (Step 2.3) — it does not constitute a second gate; no additional findings or scoring changes are made here.*\n\nThe per-file finding sections (sections 1–7 below) were written by Stage 2 per-file flushes. Stage 3 does not recreate them. Stage 3 appends only the status markers in steps 8–9, then the verdict sections in Steps 3.2–3.7.\n\n**Commit/patch header** — already in the document (written by Stage 2 first-file flush). Verify it is present; if missing, prepend it now using Stage 1 Step 1.0 data. (Stage 1 Step 1.0 data — commit SHA, author, subject, date, file counts — is sourced exclusively from context memory recorded during Stage 1; no new `read_file` or `run_in_terminal` calls are permitted here, consistent with the Stage 3 Invariant.)\n\n| Field | Commit mode source | Patch mode source |\n|-------|------------------|-------------------|\n| Review Date | Current date | Current date |\n| Author | `git show --format=\"%an <%ae>\"` | `N/A` |\n| Commit SHA | `git show --format=\"%H\"` | `N/A` |\n| Subject | `git show --format=\"%s\"` | Patch filename |\n| Files changed | Stage 1 inventory count | Stage 1 inventory count |\n\n> **Folder diff input:** Author = `Folder diff: <FOLDER_DIFF_ORG> → <FOLDER_DIFF_MOD>` · Commit SHA = `<FOLDER_DIFF_HASH>` · Subject = `<N> files: <MODIFIED> MODIFIED, <NEW> NEW, <DELETED> DELETED` · all sourced from context memory recorded in Stage 1 Step 1.0 — substitute these values for `N/A` in the above fields when input type is folder diff.\n\n**Must** verify the following sections are present in the document (already written by Stage 2). If any are missing, **must** reconstruct from Stage 1 cache and agent state before proceeding — **must not** skip reconstruction and advance to Step 3.1:\n\n1. **Commit/patch header** — table above\n2. **Agent State block** — running totals from context memory (update to final Stage 2 counts)\n3. **File inventory table** — from Stage 1 cache (`| # | File | Status | +Lines | −Lines | INF | MODULE_TYPE | Priority |`)\n4. **Full CC Registry** — maintained in `cc_registry.md` only; **not** duplicated in `Detailed_Review_Report.md` (see Step 2.0 first-file flush). Verify `cc_registry.md` exists and all `Review Status` values are populated.\n5. **Per-file findings sections** — one `## File #N: <path>` section per file, each terminated with `<!-- end-file-N -->`. Within each section: CRITICAL/MAJOR/MINOR findings in severity order, then `#### Informational Notes` for INFO items. **Every finding must include a `Fix / Improvement / Enhancement` line.**\n6. **Cross-File Findings section** (`## Cross-File Findings`) — from Step 2.2, if any, with `<!-- end-cross-file -->` anchor at the end.\n7. *(Stage 3 appends below this point)*\n\n**Must** append in order using `replace_string_in_file` — **must not** use `create_file` for these appends:\n\n8. `Stage 1 Status: ✅ COMPLETE`\n9. `Stage 2 Status: ✅ COMPLETE`\n\n### Step 3.1 — Consistency Verification\n\n**Must** perform checks 3.1.0–3.1.8 in order. If any inconsistency is detected, **must** correct `Detailed_Review_Report.md` via targeted `replace_string_in_file` operations before advancing to Step 3.2. **Must not** use `create_file` for corrections — it overwrites the entire document.\n\n#### Step 3.1.0 — Cross-Chunk Correlation\n\n**Applies only when `[CHUNK-PLAN: REGISTERED]`. Must execute before Step 3.1.1. Skip and record `[CROSS-CHUNK-CORRELATION: SKIPPED — no chunk plan]` when not applicable.**\n\nScan `Detailed_Review_Report.md` for all findings tagged `[CROSS-CHUNK: C<A>↔C<B>]`. For each:\n1. Locate the counterpart chunk's already-flushed section in `Detailed_Review_Report.md` — no new `read_file` source reads; use only the flushed report content already on disk.\n2. **Evidence confirms finding** → upgrade `Confidence: MEDIUM → HIGH`; update IDV Step 2 note to `[CROSS-CHUNK-RESOLVED: confirmed via C<B> report section]`.\n3. **Evidence refutes finding** → mark IDV verdict `FALSE POSITIVE`; increment `FP: N` in Agent State; tag `[CROSS-CHUNK-RESOLVED: refuted via C<B> report section]`.\n4. **Evidence absent or inconclusive** → retain `Confidence: MEDIUM`; tag `[CROSS-CHUNK-UNRESOLVED: manual verification recommended]`. Do not escalate severity.\n\nRecord `[CROSS-CHUNK-CORRELATION: <N> findings evaluated — <X> confirmed, <Y> refuted, <Z> unresolved]` in context memory.\n\n> **Stage 3 Invariant compliance:** No new source reads are performed — only the already-flushed `Detailed_Review_Report.md` sections are consulted.\n\n#### Step 3.1.1 — Authoritative Recount\n\n**Must** scan all finding IDs in the review document. To recount: search for all lines matching the pattern `**Severity:** [CRITICAL|MAJOR|MINOR|INFO]` in `Detailed_Review_Report.md` — each match is one finding; the severity value on that line is authoritative for scoring. `cc_registry.md` Review Status is the authoritative completeness source (no `NA` entries should remain). **Must** classify by severity and compute scores per Step 3.2. This count is authoritative — **must** reconcile with Agent State. Any discrepancy → **must** correct before proceeding.\n\n#### Step 3.1.2 — Review Status Verification\n\n**Must** verify 0 `[NOT REVIEWED]` CC entries remain in `cc_registry.md`. Any remaining → **must** return to Stage 2 for those CCs.\n\n#### Step 3.1.3 — Finding ID Continuity\n\nFinding ID uniqueness and continuity — no duplicates **and no gaps**: IDs must form a strict monotonic sequence F1, F2, F3… with no skipped numbers. If a gap is detected: when the total gap count ≤ 5, **must** renumber all findings and **must** update every cross-reference. Renumbering **must** be applied atomically to both `Detailed_Review_Report.md` (finding headings, IDV blocks, and cross-references) and `Review_Report.md` (F-ID table cells and heading anchor links) — update `Detailed_Review_Report.md` first, then `Review_Report.md` last to avoid a window where links point to missing anchors. When the total gap count > 5, record `[ID-GAP-DETECTED: F<N>..F<M> — deferred to human post-processing]` under Stage 3 Known Limitations and continue — large-scale renumbering risks document corruption. Enforce monotonic ID assignment during Stage 2 to avoid gaps in the first place.\n\n#### Step 3.1.4 — Abort Trigger Confirmation\n\nRe-confirm all abort triggers identified in Stage 2 are still recorded and correctly classified.\n\n#### Step 3.1.5 — Determinism Log\n\n*(Re-review only — skip on first-time reviews.)*\n\n`| Finding ID | Severity | Confidence | File | Description |` for all CRITICAL and MAJOR. Enables variance auditing across re-reviews. Emit only when the user explicitly requests determinism tracking. Otherwise record `[DETERMINISM-LOG: SKIPPED]`.\n\n#### Step 3.1.6 — FP Reconciliation\n\nCount IDV `<details>` blocks with verdict `FALSE POSITIVE` → must equal `FP: N` in Agent State. Mismatch → correct `FP: N` in the document header before proceeding.\n\n#### Step 3.1.7 — INF-DELTA Late-Finding Promotion *(INF-DELTA Pipeline — Stage 3: Promote & Assign Severity)*\n\n*(→ Stage 3 Invariant — all three constraints apply before opening any late finding)*\n\nFor every INF-DELTA entry from Step 1.2 (1c) that Stage 2 did not promote to a finding:\n- Open it as `[STAGE-3-LATE-FINDING: INF-DELTA]` and append to the relevant per-file section using the finding format from Phase C\n- Apply the §13 INF-DELTA three-tier severity: CRITICAL → security-critical missing dependency (auth/SMM dispatch/capsule); MAJOR → default; MINOR (Confidence: LOW) → call path proven unreachable in Stage 2\n- Entries tagged `[INF-DELTA-SCOPE: context]` may be promoted only as LOW-confidence findings unless Stage 2 recorded `[INF-DELTA-CONTEXT-REACHABLE: <API> called in <FunctionName>]` in context memory (via Phase C step 4)\n- Condensed IDV block: Step 1 = INF already read in Stage 1 (no re-read); Step 2 = `N/A — no new searches in Stage 3`; Step 3 = classify from Stage 1 cache only\n\n#### Step 3.1.8 — Fix Completeness\n\nEvery finding must have a populated `Fix / Improvement / Enhancement` field (or `Suggestion:` for INFO). Any missing → add before advancing.\n\n### Step 3.2 — Score\n\n| Severity | Base | P1/P2 file (×2) | LOW-confidence cap |\n|----------|------|-----------------|--------------------|\n| ABORT TRIGGER | 10 (treated as CRITICAL) | 20 | N/A — Abort Triggers are always HIGH confidence |\n| CRITICAL | 10 | 20 | capped: scores as MINOR base (1 pt); P1/P2 ×2 still applies → 2 pts on P1/P2 |\n| MAJOR | 5 | 10 | capped: scores as MINOR base (1 pt); P1/P2 ×2 still applies → 2 pts on P1/P2 |\n| MINOR | 1 | 2 | unchanged |\n| INFO | 0 | 0 | unchanged |\n\n> Scoring formula: `score = base × (2 if P1/P2 else 1)`, with LOW-confidence CRITICAL/MAJOR substituting base=1 before the multiplier. Abort Trigger findings always produce a BLOCK verdict (Row 1); their score is computed for completeness only.\n\n> **Confidence rule for verdicting:** LOW-confidence CRITICAL/MAJOR findings still appear in the report, but they should not drive a BLOCK verdict on their own unless they are Abort Triggers or accompanied by at least one CONFIRMED CRITICAL/MAJOR finding.\n\n**Recount procedure:** Use Step 3.1 authoritative counts. If discrepancy exists, correct Agent State before proceeding.\n\n### Step 3.3 — Verdict\n\n**Gate:** Step 3.2 recount procedure must show zero score discrepancy before advancing.\n\nFirst match, top-to-bottom:\n\n| # | Condition | Verdict |\n|---|-----------|---------|\n| 1 | Abort trigger activated | 🛑 **BLOCK** |\n| 2 | CONFIRMED CRITICAL ≥ 1 (Confidence ≥ MEDIUM) | 🔴 **BLOCK** – Fix all CRITICAL |\n| 3 | CONFIRMED MAJOR ≥ 3 (Confidence ≥ MEDIUM) | 🔴 **BLOCK** – High MAJOR density |\n| 4 | CONFIRMED MAJOR ≥ 1 (Confidence ≥ MEDIUM) AND Score ≥ 20 | 🔴 **BLOCK** – High combined risk |\n| 5 | Any P1 file skipped (regardless of total skip %) | 🔴 **BLOCK** – Critical security file unreviewed |\n| 6 | Skipped > 10% AND skipped P2 count ≥ 1 | 🔴 **BLOCK** – High-priority files unreviewed |\n| 7 | Skipped > 10% | 🟡 **CONDITIONAL APPROVE** – Insufficient coverage |\n| 8 | CRITICAL = 0, CONFIRMED MAJOR ≥ 1 (Confidence ≥ MEDIUM), Score ≤ 19 | 🟡 **CONDITIONAL APPROVE** – Fix MAJOR before merge |\n| 9 | CRITICAL = 0, CONFIRMED MAJOR = 0, Score 8–15 | 🟡 **CONDITIONAL APPROVE** – High MINOR density |\n| 10 | CRITICAL = 0, CONFIRMED MAJOR = 0, Score ≤ 7 | 🟢 **APPROVE** – MINOR tracked post-merge |\n\n> **LOW-confidence CRITICAL/MAJOR in Rows 2–4:** LOW-confidence findings (Confidence = LOW) do not satisfy the `Confidence ≥ MEDIUM` condition in Rows 2–4 and therefore cannot trigger a BLOCK verdict on their own. They still appear in the report and are scored per Step 3.2 (base=1), but they advance to Row 8/9/10 for verdicting unless accompanied by at least one CONFIRMED CRITICAL/MAJOR with Confidence ≥ MEDIUM. Abort Triggers (Row 1) are always HIGH confidence and are exempt from this rule.\n\n> **Row 3 vs Row 4 interaction:** Row 3 requires CONFIRMED MAJOR ≥ 3 regardless of score. Row 4 requires CONFIRMED MAJOR ≥ 1 AND Score ≥ 20. With exactly 2 CONFIRMED MAJORs on P1 files (score = 20): Row 3 does not fire — Row 4 fires (🔴 BLOCK). With 1 CONFIRMED MAJOR on a P1 file (score = 10): neither Row 3 nor Row 4 fires — Row 8 applies (🟡 CONDITIONAL APPROVE).\n\n### Step 3.4 — Mandatory Fix / Improvement / Enhancement List\n\n**Must** write one row per CRITICAL and MAJOR. CRITICAL first, then MAJOR, sorted by file priority. **Fix / Improvement / Enhancement** must be a concrete verb phrase (e.g., `Validate CommBufferSize >= sizeof(COMM_BUFFER) before cast at line 47`). Mark `[ARCH-CHANGE REQUIRED]` when redesign is needed; mark `[ENHANCEMENT]` for hardening improvements that are non-blocking. If none: `> No mandatory fixes.`\n\n| Priority | Finding ID | File | Severity | Description | Fix / Improvement / Enhancement |\n|----------|------------|------|----------|-------------|------------|\n\n### Step 3.5 — Post-Merge Tracking\n\n**Must** write one row per MINOR/INFO. Target: `post-merge` / `next sprint` / `tracking only`. Omit section entirely if no MINOR/INFO findings exist. MINOR-B findings are copied directly from Stage 2 without re-evaluation — mark the `Target` column `tracking only` and the `Severity` column `MINOR-B` to distinguish them from MINOR-A items.\n\n### Step 3.6 — Known Limitations\n\n**Must** list any of: unverified spec citations, ELINK prototypes not found, skipped files, incomplete call graphs, `[CLEANUP-FAILED]`, `[CHECKPOINT-WRITE-SKIPPED]`. **Must** include this section whenever any limitation was recorded during the review. Omit only when no limitations exist.\n\n### Step 3.7 — Final Output\n\nWrite to `.github/reviews/AI_Review_Report-<REPORT_PREFIX><TIMESTAMP>.md` (use the filename computed in Step 0.2A). Sections: commit/patch header (SHA, Author, Date, Subject) → VERDICT → SCORE → Coverage → Files Reviewed → Issues (omit if none) → Known Limitations (omit if none) → link to full review.\n\nPrefer a findings-first summary layout in `AI_Review_Report-<REPORT_PREFIX><TIMESTAMP>.md`: VERDICT → mandatory fixes → score and coverage → key limitations → link to full review. Keep the full inventory and CC registry in `AI_Detailed_Review_Report-<REPORT_PREFIX><TIMESTAMP>.md`.\n\n**`Review_Report.md` template:**\n\n> **Location link format** — identical to `Detailed_Review_Report.md`. Both files reside in `.github/reviews/`, so the `../../` prefix reaches workspace root:\n> `` `FunctionName()` [<basename>:<line>](../../<repo-root-relative/path/file.c>#L<line>) ``\n> Example: `` `FooHandler()` [Bar.c:47](../../AmiModulePkg/Foo/Bar.c#L47) ``\n> Populate from the **Resolved Line** already recorded in the CC Registry — do not re-derive.\n\n```markdown\n# Review Report — <subject> (<sha8>)\n**Date:** <YYYY-MM-DD> | **Author:** <name or N/A> | **Verdict:** <🛑/🔴/🟡/🟢 VERDICT TEXT>\n**Reviewed by:** <model-name-and-version> · UEFI Code Review Agent v1.4| **Source:**
